Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the hyperbolic framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 965. The resulting operator achieves bound O(76/n^3).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_965(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 965. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^76(S, F) is trivial. QED.
